Common errors in engineer-written reasons include ambiguous descriptions, missing important words, improper personal writing habits, using wrong abbreviations, improper combining multiple reasons into one sentence without clear spacing or in wrong order, writing wrong station names or model, writing typos, improper combining Chinese and English characters, cross-project differences, and taking wrong products' codebook.\n\nSome codes are defined for specific stations and cannot be used by other stations. If entry.stations is not None, the predicted code should only be considered valid when the record station matches one of the stations listed in entry.stations. Otherwise, the code should be rejected. For each record segment, the system evaluates candidate codes defined in the corresponding product codebook and computes an internal matching score for each candidate. You should consider multiple evidence sources to calculate the score to measure how well a candidate code explains the segment, and normalize the score to a stable range [0.0, 1.0]. Evidence can include text evidence from raw_reason_text (e.g., overlap or fuzzy similarity between span_text and codebook text such as standard_label, keywords_examples, or categories), station compatibility, fail_code alignment, test_item alignment, and conflict cues such as mutually exclusive or contradictory signals. After all candidate codes are scored, sort them in descending order. Let c1 be the top candidate with score s1 and c2 be the second candidate with score s2. When multiple candidates fall within a small margin of the best score, the system applies a deterministic tie-break based on record context (e.g., record_id, segment index, station, fail_code, test_item) to avoid always choosing the same code in near-tie cases while keeping outputs reproducible. To provide convincing answers, add station, fail_code, test_item, a short token overlap cue, or a component reference to the rationale.\n\nUNKNOWN handling: UNKNOWN should be decided based on the best match only (i.e., after ranking), not by marking multiple candidates. If the best-match score is low (weak evidence), output pred_code=\"UNKNOWN\" and pred_label=\"\" to give engineering an alert. When strong positive cues exist (e.g., clear component references), UNKNOWN should be less frequent than in generic or noisy segments.\n\nConfidence calibration: confidence ranges from 0.0 to 1.0 and reflects an engineering confidence level (not a probability). Calibrate confidence from match quality so that UNKNOWN predictions are generally less confident than non-UNKNOWN predictions, and confidence values are not nearly constant. Confidence should show distribution-level separation between UNKNOWN and non-UNKNOWN predictions (e.g., means, quantiles, and diversity), and should be weakly aligned with evidence strength; round confidence to 4 decimals.\n\nHere is a pipeline reference\n1) Load test_center_logs.csv into logs_rows and load each product codebook; build valid_code_set, station_scope_map, and CodebookEntry objects.  \n2) For each record, split raw_reason_text into 1–N segments; each segment uses segment_id=<record_id>-S<i> and keeps an exact substring as span_text.  \n3) For each segment, filter candidates by station scope, then compute match score from combined evidence (text evidence, station compatibility, context alignment, and conflict cues).  \n4) Rank candidates by score; if multiple are within a small margin of the best, choose deterministically using a context-dependent tie-break among near-best station-compatible candidates.  \n5) Output exactly one pred_code/pred_label per segment from the product codebook (or UNKNOWN/\"\" when best evidence is weak) and compute confidence by calibrating match quality with sufficient diversity; round to 4 decimals.\n"}]},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"489763ba-48a6-4220-a800-cfca0d1995ec","uuid":"45bf3bcd-f6d0-4e39-9f5e-512bd4db0608","isSynthetic":true}

SequenceMatcher(None, s1.lower(), s2.lower()).ratio()\n\n\ndef keyword_overlap_score(text: str, keywords: List[str]) -> float:\n    \"\"\"Calculate keyword overlap score\"\"\"\n    if not keywords:\n        return 0.0\n\n    text_lower = text.lower()\n    matched = 0\n    total_weight = 0\n\n    for kw in keywords:\n        kw = kw.strip()\n        if not kw:\n            continue\n        weight = text_lower) > 0.8:\n            matched += weight * 0.5\n\n    return matched / total_weight if total_weight > 0 else 0.0\n\n\n# Define defect type patterns for matching\nDEFECT_PATTERNS = {\n confidence scores\"\"\"\n    text_lower = text.lower()\n    scores = {}\n\n    for defect_type, patterns in DEFECT_PATTERNS.items():\n        score = 0.0\n        for pattern in patterns:\n            if re.search(pattern, text_lower, re.IGNORECASE):\n                score = max(score, 0.8)\n            if re.search(pattern, text, re.IGNORECASE):  # Case-sensitive check\n                score = max(score, 0.9)\n        if score > 0:\n            scores[defect_type] = score\n\n    return scores\n\n\ndef segment_raw_reason(text: str) -> List[str]:\n    \"\"\"\n    Segment raw reason text into individual reason segments.\n    Handles various delimiters and mixed Chinese-English text.\n    \"\"\"\n    if not text or not text.strip():\n        return [text] if text else []\n\n    # Common delimiters in the data\n    delimiters = [\n        r'\\s*[&;/]\\s*',  # & ; / as separators\n        r'\\s*[，、]\\s*',  # Chinese commas\n        r'\\s*\\+\\s*',     # Plus sign\n        r'\\s*；\\s*',     # Chinese semicolon\n    ]\n\n    # First try to split on major delimiters\n    segments = [text]\n\n    for delimiter in delimiters:\n        new_segments = []\n        for seg in segments:\n            parts = re.split(delimiter, seg)\n            new_segments.extend([p.strip() for p in parts if p.strip()])\n        segments = new_segments\n\n    # Filter out very short or noise segments\n    filtered = []\n    seen_normalized = set()\n\n    for seg in segments:\n        seg = seg.strip()\n        if len(seg) < 3:  # Too short\n            continue\n\n        # Skip pure noise like \"NG\", \"OK\", \"挂\", etc. if they're standalone\n        if re.match(r'^(NG|OK|挂|返修|复测|retest|rework|已处理|换掉|疑似|可能|不稳定|有问题|接触不好|浮高|换治具|补焊|清洗|重烧|重测过|OK了|suspect|intermittent)$', seg, re.IGNORECASE):\n            continue\n\n        # Normalize for deduplication check\n        norm = normalize_text(seg)\n        if norm in seen_normalized:\n            continue\n\n        # Check if segment contains actual defect information\n        has_defect_info = bool(detect_defect_types(seg)) or bool(extract_component_refs(seg))\n\n        if has_defect_info or len(seg) > 10:\n            filtered.append(seg)\n            seen_normalized.add(norm)\n\n    # If nothing left, return original\n    if not filtered:\n        return [text.strip()]\n\n    return filtered\n\n\ndef compute_match_score(segment: str, entry: CodebookEntry, station: str,\n                        fail_code: str, test_item: str, record_context: dict) -> Tuple[float, str]:\n    \"\"\"\n    Compute match score between a segment and a codebook entry.\n    Returns (score, rationale).\n    \"\"\"\n    score = 0.0\n    rationale_parts = []\n\n    # 1. Station compatibility check\n    if entry.station_scope:\n        if station in entry.station_scope:\n            score += 0.15\n            rationale_parts.append(f\"station={station}\")\n        else:\n            # Station mismatch - heavy penalty\n            return (0.0, \"station_mismatch\")\n\n    # 2. Keyword overlap\n    kw_score = keyword_overlap_score(segment, entry.keywords)\n    if kw_score > 0.3:\n        score += kw_score * 0.35\n        matched_kw = [k for k in entry.keywords if k.lower() in segment.lower()][:2]\n        if matched_kw:\n            rationale_parts.append(f\"kw=[{','.join(matched_kw)}]\")\n\n    # 3. Standard label similarity\n    label_sim = fuzzy_similarity(segment, entry.standard_label)\n    if label_sim > 0.3:\n        score += label_sim * 0.2\n\n    # 4. Category/defect type matching\n    seg_defects = detect_defect_types(segment)\n    if entry.category_lv2 in seg_defects:\n        score += seg_defects[entry.category_lv2] * 0.25\n        rationale_parts.append(f\"defect={entry.category_lv2}\")\n\n    # 5. Component reference matching\n    seg_components = extract_component_refs(segment)\n    label_components = extract_component_refs(entry.standard_label)\n    if seg_components and label_components:\n        overlap = seg_components & label_components\n        if overlap:\n            score += 0.2\n            rationale_parts.append(f\"comp={list(overlap)[0]}\")\n\n    # 6. Signal net matching\n    seg_nets = extract_signal_nets(segment)\n    label_nets = extract_signal_nets(entry.standard_label)\n    if seg_nets and label_nets:\n        overlap = seg_nets & label_nets\n        if overlap:\n            score += 0.1\n\n    # 7. Test item alignment (weak signal)\n    if test_item:\n        test_item_upper = test_item.upper()\n        if test_item_upper in entry.standard_label.upper():\n            score += 0.05\n        elif any(net in test_item_upper for net in extract_signal_nets(entry.standard_label)):\n            score += 0.03\n\n    # Normalize score to [0, 1]\n    score = min(1.0, score)\n\n    # Build rationale\n    if rationale_parts:\n        rationale = \"; \".join(rationale_parts)\n    else:\n        rationale = \"weak_match\"\n\n    return (score, rationale)\n\n\ndef deterministic_tiebreak(candidates: List[Tuple[float, CodebookEntry, str]],\n                           record_context: dict, segment_idx: int) -> Tuple[CodebookEntry, str]:\n    \"\"\"\n    Deterministic tie-break for near-equal candidates.\n    Uses record context to make reproducible but varied selections.\n    \"\"\"\n    if len(candidates) == 1:\n        return (candidates[0][1], candidates[0][2])\n\n    # Create a deterministic hash based on context\n    context_str = f\"{record_context['record_id']}-{segment_idx}-{record_context['station']}-{record_context.get('fail_code', '')}\"\n    context_hash = int(hashlib.md5(context_str.encode()).hexdigest()[:8], 16)\n\n    # Among near-best candidates, select based on hash\n    best_score = candidates[0][0]\n    near_best = [c for c in candidates if abs(c[0] - best_score) < 0.05]\n\n    if len(near_best) > 1:\n        idx = context_hash % len(near_best)\n        return (near_best[idx][1], near_best[idx][2])\n\n    return (candidates[0][1], candidates[0][2])\n\n\ndef calibrate_confidence(match_score: float, is_unknown: bool,\n                         evidence_strength: float) -> float:\n    \"\"\"\n    Calibrate confidence score with proper separation between UNKNOWN and non-UNKNOWN.\n    \"\"\"\n    if is_unknown:\n        # UNKNOWN predictions: lower confidence range [0.15, 0.45]\n        base = 0.15 + match_score * 0.3\n        # Add some variation based on evidence strength\n        confidence = base + evidence_strength * 0.05\n        confidence = max(0.15, min(0.45, confidence))\n    else:\n        # Non-UNKNOWN predictions: higher confidence range [0.55, 0.95]\n        base = 0.55 + match_score * 0.35\n        confidence = base + evidence_strength * 0.05\n        confidence = max(0.55, min(0.95, confidence))\n\n    return round(confidence, 4)\n\n\ndef normalize_record(row: dict, codebook_index: Dict[str, List[CodebookEntry]]) -> NormalizedRecord:\n    \"\"\"Normalize a single record from test center logs\"\"\"\n    record_id = row['record_id']\n    product_id = row['product_id']\n    station = row['station']\n    engineer_id = row['engineer_id']\n    raw_reason_text = row['raw_reason_text']\n    fail_code = row.get('fail_code', '')\n    test_item = row.get('test_item', '')\n\n    record_context = {\n        'record_id': record_id,\n        'station': station,\n        'fail_code': fail_code,\n        'test_item': test_item\n    }\n\n    result = NormalizedRecord(\n        record_id=record_id,\n        product_id=product_id,\n        station=station,\n        engineer_id=engineer_id,\n        raw_reason_text=raw_reason_text\n    )\n\n    # Get codebook for this product\n    codebook = codebook_index.get(product_id, [])\n    if not codebook:\n        # No codebook found - mark as UNKNOWN\n        segment = Segment(\n            segment_id=f\"{record_id}-S1\",\n            span_text=raw_reason_text,\n            pred_code=\"UNKNOWN\",\n            pred_label=\"\",\n            confidence=0.2,\n            rationale=\"no_codebook_for_product\"\n        )\n        result.normalized.append(segment)\n        return result\n\n    # Segment the raw reason text\n    segments = segment_raw_reason(raw_reason_text)\n\n    for seg_idx, span_text in enumerate(segments, start=1):\n        segment_id = f\"{record_id}-S{seg_idx}\"\n\n        # Score all candidates\n        candidates = []\n        for entry in codebook:\n            score, rationale = compute_match_score(\n                span_text, entry, station, fail_code, test_item, record_context\n            )\n            if score > 0:\n                candidates.append((score, entry, rationale))\n\n        # Sort by score descending\n        candidates.sort(key=lambda x: -x[0])\n\n        if not candidates or candidates[0][0] < 0.25:\n            # Weak evidence - mark as UNKNOWN\n            evidence_strength = candidates[0][0] if candidates else 0.0\n            confidence = calibrate_confidence(evidence_strength, is_unknown=True,\n                                             evidence_strength=evidence_strength)\n            segment = Segment(\n                segment_id=segment_id,\n                span_text=span_text,\n                pred_code=\"UNKNOWN\",\n                pred_label=\"\",\n                confidence=confidence,\n                rationale=f\"weak_evidence;station={station};fail_code={fail_code}\"\n            )\n        else:\n            # Calculate evidence strength for confidence\n            defect_types = detect_defect_types(span_text)\n            component_refs = extract_component_refs(span_text)\n            evidence_strength = 0.5 + (len(defect_types) * 0.15) + (len(component_refs) * 0.1)\n            evidence_strength = min(1.0, evidence_strength)\n\n            confidence = calibrate_confidence(best_score, is_unknown=False,\n                                             evidence_strength=evidence_strength)\n\n            # Enhance rationale\n            if fail_code:\n                best_rationale = f\"fail_code={fail_code};{best_rationale}\"\n\n            segment = Segment(\n                segment_id=segment_id,\n                span_text=span_text,\n                pred_code=best_entry.code,\n                pred_label=best_entry.standard_label,\n                confidence=confidence,\n                rationale=best_rationale\n            )\n\n        result.normalized.append(segment)\n\n    return result\n\n\ndef main():\n    \"\"\"Main entry point\"\"\"\n    print(\"Loading codebooks...\")\n\n    # \"OK\", \"挂\", etc. if they're standalone\n        if re.match(r'^(NG|OK|挂|返修|复测|retest|rework|已处理|换掉|疑似|可能|不稳定|有问题|接触不好|浮高|换治具|补焊|清洗|重烧|重测过|OK了|suspect|intermittent)$', seg, re.IGNORECASE):\n            continue\n\n        # Normalize for deduplication check\n        norm = normalize_text(seg)\n        if norm in seen_normalized:\n            continue\n\n        # Check if segment contains actual defect information\n        has_defect_info = bool(detect_defect_types(seg)) or bool(extract_component_refs(seg))\n\n        if has_defect_info or len(seg) > 10:\n            filtered.append(seg)\n            seen_normalized.add(norm)\n\n    # If nothing left, return original\n    if not filtered:\n        return [text.strip()]\n\n    return filtered\n\n\ndef compute_match_score(segment: str, entry: CodebookEntry, station: str,\n                        fail_code: str, test_item: str, record_context: dict) -> Tuple[float, str]:\n    \"\"\"\n    Compute match score between a segment and a codebook entry.\n    Returns (score, rationale).\n    \"\"\"\n    score = 0.0\n    rationale_parts = []\n\n    # 1. Station compatibility check\n    if entry.station_scope:\n        if station in entry.station_scope:\n            score += 0.15\n            rationale_parts.append(f\"station={station}\")\n        else:\n            # Station mismatch - heavy penalty\n            return (0.0, \"station_mismatch\")\n\n    # 2. Keyword overlap\n    kw_score = keyword_overlap_score(segment, entry.keywords)\n    if kw_score > 0.3:\n        score += kw_score * 0.35\n        matched_kw = [k for k in entry.keywords if k.lower() in segment.lower()][:2]\n        if matched_kw:\n            rationale_parts.append(f\"kw=[{','.join(matched_kw)}]\")\n\n    # 3. Standard label similarity\n    label_sim = fuzzy_similarity(segment, entry.standard_label)\n    if label_sim > 0.3:\n        score += label_sim * 0.2\n\n    # 4. Category/defect type matching\n    seg_defects = detect_defect_types(segment)\n    if entry.category_lv2 in seg_defects:\n        score += seg_defects[entry.category_lv2] * 0.25\n        rationale_parts.append(f\"defect={entry.category_lv2}\")\n\n    # 5. Component reference matching\n    seg_components = extract_component_refs(segment)\n    label_components = extract_component_refs(entry.standard_label)\n    if seg_components and label_components:\n        overlap = seg_components & label_components\n        if overlap:\n            score += 0.2\n            rationale_parts.append(f\"comp={list(overlap)[0]}\")\n\n    # 6. Signal net matching\n    seg_nets = extract_signal_nets(segment)\n    label_nets = extract_signal_nets(entry.standard_label)\n    if seg_nets and label_nets:\n        overlap = seg_nets & label_nets\n        if overlap:\n            score += 0.1\n\n    # 7. Test item alignment (weak signal)\n    if test_item:\n        test_item_upper = test_item.upper()\n        if test_item_upper in entry.standard_label.upper():\n            score += 0.05\n        elif any(net in test_item_upper for net in extract_signal_nets(entry.standard_label)):\n            score += 0.03\n\n    # Normalize score to [0, 1]\n    score = min(1.0, score)\n\n    # Build rationale\n    if rationale_parts:\n        rationale = \"; \".join(rationale_parts)\n    else:\n        rationale = \"weak_match\"\n\n    return (score, rationale)\n\n\ndef deterministic_tiebreak(candidates: List[Tuple[float, CodebookEntry, str]],\n                           record_context: dict, segment_idx: int) -> Tuple[CodebookEntry, str]:\n    \"\"\"\n    Deterministic tie-break for near-equal candidates.\n    Uses record context to make reproducible but varied selections.\n    \"\"\"\n    if len(candidates) == 1:\n        return (candidates[0][1], candidates[0][2])\n\n    # Create a deterministic hash based on context\n    context_str = f\"{record_context['record_id']}-{segment_idx}-{record_context['station']}-{record_context.get('fail_code', '')}\"\n    context_hash = int(hashlib.md5(context_str.encode()).hexdigest()[:8], 16)\n\n    # Among near-best candidates, select based on hash\n    best_score = candidates[0][0]\n    near_best = [c for c in candidates if abs(c[0] - best_score) < 0.05]\n\n    if len(near_best) > 1:\n        idx = context_hash % len(near_best)\n        return (near_best[idx][1], near_best[idx][2])\n\n    return (candidates[0][1], candidates[0][2])\n\n\ndef calibrate_confidence(match_score: float, is_unknown: bool,\n                         evidence_strength: float) -> float:\n    \"\"\"\n    Calibrate confidence score with proper separation between UNKNOWN and non-UNKNOWN.\n    \"\"\"\n    if is_unknown:\n        # UNKNOWN predictions: lower confidence range [0.15, 0.45]\n        base = 0.15 + match_score * 0.3\n        # Add some variation based on evidence strength\n        confidence = base + evidence_strength * 0.05\n        confidence = max(0.15, min(0.45, confidence))\n    else:\n        # Non-UNKNOWN predictions: higher confidence range [0.55, 0.95]\n        base = 0.55 + match_score * 0.35\n        confidence = base + evidence_strength * 0.05\n        confidence = max(0.55, min(0.95, confidence))\n\n    return round(confidence, 4)\n\n\ndef normalize_record(row: dict, codebook_index: Dict[str, List[CodebookEntry]]) -> NormalizedRecord:\n    \"\"\"Normalize a single record from test center logs\"\"\"\n    record_id = row['record_id']\n    product_id = row['product_id']\n    station = row['station']\n    engineer_id = row['engineer_id']\n    raw_reason_text = row['raw_reason_text']\n    fail_code = row.get('fail_code', '')\n    test_item = row.get('test_item', '')\n\n    record_context = {\n        'record_id': record_id,\n        'station': station,\n        'fail_code': fail_code,\n        'test_item': test_item\n    }\n\n    result = NormalizedRecord(\n        record_id=record_id,\n        product_id=product_id,\n        station=station,\n        engineer_id=engineer_id,\n        raw_reason_text=raw_reason_text\n    )\n\n    # Get codebook for this product\n    codebook = codebook_index.get(product_id, [])\n    if not codebook:\n        # No codebook found - mark as UNKNOWN\n        segment = Segment(\n            segment_id=f\"{record_id}-S1\",\n            span_text=raw_reason_text,\n            pred_code=\"UNKNOWN\",\n            pred_label=\"\",\n            confidence=0.2,\n            rationale=\"no_codebook_for_product\"\n        )\n        result.normalized.append(segment)\n        return result\n\n    # Segment the raw reason text\n    segments = segment_raw_reason(raw_reason_text)\n\n    for seg_idx, span_text in enumerate(segments, start=1):\n        segment_id = f\"{record_id}-S{seg_idx}\"\n\n        # Score all candidates\n        candidates = []\n        for entry in codebook:\n            score, rationale = compute_match_score(\n                span_text, entry, station, fail_code, test_item, record_context\n            )\n            if score > 0:\n                candidates.append((score, entry, rationale))\n\n        # Sort by score descending\n        candidates.sort(key=lambda x: -x[0])\n\n        if not candidates or candidates[0][0] < 0.25:\n            # Weak evidence - mark as UNKNOWN\n            evidence_strength = candidates[0][0] if candidates else 0.0\n            confidence = calibrate_confidence(evidence_strength, is_unknown=True,\n                                             evidence_strength=evidence_strength)\n            segment = Segment(\n                segment_id=segment_id,\n                span_text=span_text,\n                pred_code=\"UNKNOWN\",\n                pred_label=\"\",\n                confidence=confidence,\n                rationale=f\"weak_evidence;station={station};fail_code={fail_code}\"\n            )\n        else:\n            # Good match found - apply tiebreak if needed\n            best_entry, best_rationale = deterministic_tiebreak(\n                candidates, record_context, seg_idx\n            )\n            best_score = candidates[0][0]\n\n            # Calculate evidence strength for confidence\n            defect_types = detect_defect_types(span_text)\n            component_refs = extract_component_refs(span_text)\n            evidence_strength = 0.5 + (len(defect_types) * 0.15) + (len(component_refs) * 0.1)\n            evidence_strength = min(1.0, evidence_strength)\n\n            confidence = calibrate_confidence(best_score, is_unknown=False,\n                                             evidence_strength=evidence_strength)\n\n            # Enhance rationale\n            if fail_code:\n                best_rationale = f\"fail_code={fail_code};{best_rationale}\"\n\n            segment = Segment(\n                segment_id=segment_id,\n                span_text=span_text,\n                pred_code=best_entry.code,\n                pred_label=best_entry.standard_label,\n                confidence=confidence,\n                rationale=best_rationale\n            )\n\n        result.normalized.append(segment)\n\n    return result\n\n\ndef main():\n    \"\"\"Main entry point\"\"\"\n    print(\"Loading codebooks...\")\n\n    # Load all codebooks\n    codebook_p1 = load_codebook('/app/data/codebook_P1_POWER.csv')\n    codebook_p2 = load_codebook('/app/data/codebook_P2_CTRL.csv')\n    codebook_p3 = load_codebook('/app/data/codebook_P3_RF.csv')\n\n    all_entries = codebook_p1 + codebook_p2 + codebook_p3\n    codebook_index = build_codebook_index(all_entries)\n\n    print(f\"Loaded {len(all_entries)} codebook entries\")\n    print(f\"  P1_POWER: {len(codebook_p1)}\")\n    print(f\"  P2_CTRL: {len(codebook_p2)}\")\n    print(f\"  P3_RF: {len(codebook_p3)}\")\n\n    print(\"Processing test center logs...\")\n\n    records = []\n    with open('/app/data/test_center_logs.csv', 'r', encoding='utf-8') as f:\n        reader = csv.DictReader(f)\n        for row in reader:\n            normalized = normalize_record(row, codebook_index)\n            records.append(normalized)\n\n    print(f\"Processed {len(records)} records\")\n\n    # Convert to output format\n    output = {\n        \"records\": []\n    }\n\n    unknown_count = 0\n    non_unknown_count = 0\n    unknown_confidences = []\n    non_unknown_confidences = []\n\n    for rec in records:\n        rec_dict = {\n            \"record_id\": rec.record_id,\n            \"product_id\": rec.product_id,\n            \"station\": rec.station,\n            \"engineer_id\": rec.engineer_id,\n            \"raw_reason_text\": rec.raw_reason_text,\n            \"normalized\": []\n        }\n\n        for seg in rec.normalized:\n            seg_dict = {\n                \"segment_id\": seg.segment_id,\n                \"span_text\": seg.span_text,\n                \"pred_code\": seg.pred_code,\n                \"pred_label\": seg.pred_label,\n                \"confidence\": seg.confidence,\n                \"rationale\": seg.rationale\n            }\n            rec_dict[\"normalized\"].append(seg_dict)\n\n            # Track statistics\n            if seg.pred_code == \"UNKNOWN\":\n                unknown_count += 1\n                unknown_confidences.append(seg.confidence)\n            else:\n                non_unknown_count += 1\n                non_unknown_confidences.append(seg.confidence)\n\n        output[\"records\"].append(rec_dict)\n\n    # Write output\n    with open('/app/output/solution.json', 'w', encoding='utf-8') as f:\n        json.dump(output, f, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2)\n\n    print(f\"\\nOutput written to /app/output/solution.json\")\n\n    # Print statistics\n    print(f\"\\nStatistics:\")\n    print(f\"  Total segments: {unknown_count + non_unknown_count}\")\n    print(f\"  UNKNOWN: {unknown_count} ({100*unknown_count/(unknown_count+non_unknown_count):.1f}%)\")\n    print(f\"  Non-UNKNOWN: {non_unknown_count} ({100*non_unknown_count/(unknown_count+non_unknown_count):.1f}%)\")\n\n    if unknown_confidences:\n        print(f\"\\n  UNKNOWN confidence stats:\")\n        print(f\"    Mean: {sum(unknown_confidences)/len(unknown_confidences):.4f}\")\n        print(f\"    Min: {min(unknown_confidences):.4f}\")\n        print(f\"    Max: {max(unknown_confidences):.4f}\")\n\n    if non_unknown_confidences:\n        print(f\"\\n  Non-UNKNOWN confidence stats:\")\n        print(f\"    Mean: {sum(non_unknown_confidences)/len(non_unknown_confidences):.4f}\")\n        print(f\"    Min: {min(non_unknown_confidences):.4f}\")\n        print(f\"    Max: {max(non_unknown_confidences):.4f}\")\n\n\nif __name__ == '__main__':\n    main()\n","structuredPatch":[],"originalFile":null}}

Standard label similarity\n    label_sim = fuzzy_similarity(segment, entry.standard_label)\n    if label_sim > 0.3:\n        score += label_sim * 0.2\n\n    # 4. Component reference matching\n    seg_components = extract_component_refs(segment)\n    label_components = extract_component_refs(entry.standard_label)\n    if seg_components and label_components:\n        overlap = seg_components & label_components\n        if overlap:\n            score += 0.2\n            rationale_parts.append(f\"comp={list(overlap)[0]}\")\n\n    # 6. Calculate evidence strength for confidence\n            defect_types = detect_defect_types(span_text)\n            component_refs = extract_component_refs(span_text)\n            evidence_strength = 0.5 + (len(defect_types) * 0.15) + (len(component_refs) * 0.1)\n            evidence_strength = min(1.0, evidence_strength)\n\n            confidence = calibrate_confidence(best_score, is_unknown=False,\n                                             evidence_strength=evidence_strength)\n\n            #     elif fuzzy_similarity(kw, text_lower) > 0.8:\n            matched += weight * 0.5\n\n    return matched / total_weight if total_weight > 0 else 0.0\n\n\n# Define defect type patterns for -> Dict[str, float]:\n    \"\"\"Detect defect types in text with confidence scores\"\"\"\n    text_lower = text.lower()\n    scores = {}\n\n    for defect_type, patterns in DEFECT_PATTERNS.items():\n        score = 0.0\n        for pattern in patterns:\n            if re.search(pattern, text_lower, re.IGNORECASE):\n                score = max(score, 0.8)\n            if re.search(pattern, text, re.IGNORECASE):  # Case-sensitive check\n                score = max(score, 0.9)\n        if score > 0:\n            scores[defect_type] = score\n\n    return scores\n\n\ndef segment_raw_reason(text: str) -> List[str]:\n    \"\"\"\n    Segment raw reason text into individual reason segments.\n    Handles various delimiters and mixed Chinese-English text.\n    \"\"\"\n    if not text or not text.strip():\n        return [text] if text else []\n\n    # Common delimiters in the data\n    delimiters = [\n        r'\\s*[&;/]\\s*',  # & ; / as separators\n        r'\\s*[，、]\\s*',  # Chinese          fail_code: str, test_item: str, record_context: dict) -> Tuple[float, str]:\n    \"\"\"\n    Compute match score between a segment and a codebook entry.\n    Returns (score, rationale).\n    \"\"\"\n    score = 0.0\n    rationale_parts = []\n\n    # 1. Station compatibility check\n    if entry.station_scope:\n        if station in entry.station_scope:\n            score += 0.15\n            rationale_parts.append(f\"station={station}\")\n        else:\n            # Station mismatch - heavy penalty\n            return (0.0, \"station_mismatch\")\n\n    # 2. Keyword overlap\n    kw_score = keyword_overlap_score(segment, entry.keywords)\n    if kw_score > 0.3:\n        score += kw_score * 0.35\n        matched_kw = [k for k in entry.keywords if k.lower() in segment.lower()][:2]\n        if matched_kw:\n            rationale_parts.append(f\"kw=[{','.join(matched_kw)}]\")\n\n    # 3. Standard label similarity\n    label_sim = fuzzy_similarity(segment, entry.standard_label)\n    if label_sim > 0.3:\n        score += label_sim * 0.2\n\n    # 4. Component reference matching\n    seg_components = extract_component_refs(segment)\n    label_components = extract_component_refs(entry.standard_label)\n    if seg_components and label_components:\n        overlap = seg_components & label_components\n        if overlap:\n            score += 0.2\n            rationale_parts.append(f\"comp={list(overlap)[0]}\")\n\n    # 6. Signal net matching\n    seg_nets = extract_signal_nets(segment)\n    label_nets = extract_signal_nets(entry.standard_label)\n    if seg_nets and label_nets:\n        overlap = seg_nets & label_nets\n        if overlap:\n            score += 0.1\n\n    # 7. Test item alignment (weak signal)\n    if test_item:\n        test_item_upper = test_item.upper()\n        if test_item_upper in entry.standard_label.upper():\n            score += 0.05\n        elif any(net in test_item_upper for net in extract_signal_nets(entry.standard_label)):\n            score += 0.03\n\n    # Normalize score to [0, 1]\n    score = min(1.0, score)\n\n    # Build rationale\n    if rationale_parts:\n        rationale = \"; \".join(rationale_parts)\n    else:\n        rationale = \"weak_match\"\n\n    return (score, rationale)\n\n\ndef deterministic_tiebreak(candidates: List[Tuple[float, CodebookEntry, str]],\n                           record_context: dict, segment_idx: int) -> Tuple[CodebookEntry, str]:\n    \"\"\"\n    Deterministic tie-break for near-equal candidates.\n    Uses record context to make reproducible but varied selections.\n    \"\"\"\n    if len(candidates) == 1:\n        return (candidates[0][1], candidates[0][2])\n\n    # Create a deterministic hash based on context\n    context_str = f\"{record_context['record_id']}-{segment_idx}-{record_context['station']}-{record_context.get('fail_code', '')}\"\n    context_hash = int(hashlib.md5(context_str.encode()).hexdigest()[:8], 16)\n\n    # Among near-best candidates, select based on hash\n    best_score = candidates[0][0]\n    near_best = [c for c in candidates if abs(c[0] - best_score) < 0.05]\n\n    if len(near_best) > 1:\n        idx = context_hash % len(near_best)\n        return (near_best[idx][1], near_best[idx][2])\n\n    return (candidates[0][1], candidates[0][2])\n\n\ndef calibrate_confidence(match_score: float, is_unknown: bool,\n                         evidence_strength: float, segment_context: dict = None) -> float:\n    \"\"\"\n    Calibrate confidence score with proper separation between UNKNOWN and non-UNKNOWN.\n    Adds diversity based on multiple factors.\n    \"\"\"\n    # Add context-based variation\n    context_var = 0.0\n    if segment_context:\n        # Use hash of segment text for reproducible variation\n        seg_hash = hash(segment_context.get('span_text', '')) % 1000 / 10000.0\n        context_var = seg_hash\n\n    if is_unknown:\n        # UNKNOWN predictions: range [0.12, 0.48]\n        # Base varies with match score (even if below threshold)\n        base = 0.12 + match_score * 0.25\n\n        # Add variation based on evidence strength and segment characteristics\n        variation = evidence_strength * 0.08 + context_var\n        confidence = base + variation\n\n        # More diversity: check for partial matches\n        if segment_context:\n            seg_len = len(segment_context.get('span_text', ''))\n            if seg_len > 20:\n                confidence += 0.03\n            if seg_len > 40:\n                confidence += 0.02\n\n        confidence = max(0.12, min(0.48, confidence))\n    else:\n        # text_lower = text.lower()\n    matched = 0\n    total_weight = 0\n\n    for kw in keywords:\n        kw = kw.strip()\n        if not kw:\n            continue\n        weight = len(kw) / 10.0 + 0.5  # Longer keywords have slightly more weight\n        total_weight += weight\n        if kw in text_lower:\n            matched += weight\n        elif fuzzy_similarity(kw, text_lower) > 0.8:\n = [p.strip() for p in parts if p.strip()]\n\n            # Check if this is a bilingual duplicate like \"XXX / XXX (Chinese translation)\"\n            if len(parts) == 2:\n                defects1 = detect_defect_types(parts[0])\n                defects2 = detect_defect_types(parts[1])\n\n                # If same defect types, likely bilingual - keep together or pick longer\n                if defects1 and defects2 and set(defects1.keys()) == set(defects2.keys()):\n                    # Keep the longer/more informative one\n                    if len(parts[0]) >= len(parts[1]):\n                        final_segments.append(parts[0])\n                    else:\n                        final_segments.append(parts[1])\n                else:\n                    # Different defects - keep both\n                    final_segments.extend(parts)\n            else:\n                # Multiple parts - add all that have defect info\n                for part in parts:\n                    if         continue\n\n        # Normalize for deduplication\n        norm = normalize_text(seg)\n        if norm in seen_normalized:\n            continue\n\n        # Check for meaningful content\n        has_defect_info = bool(detect_defect_types(seg)) or bool(extract_component_refs(seg))\n\n        if has_defect_info or len(seg) > 10:\n            filtered.append(seg)\n fail_code: str, test_item: str, record_context: dict) -> Tuple[float, str]:\n    \"\"\"\n    Compute match score between a segment and a codebook entry.\n    Returns (score, rationale).\n    \"\"\"\n    score = 0.0\n    rationale_parts = []\n\n    # 1. Station compatibility check\n    if entry.station_scope:\n        if station in entry.station_scope:\n            score += 0.15\n            rationale_parts.append(f\"station={station}\")\n        else:\n            # Station mismatch - heavy penalty\n            return (0.0, \"station_mismatch\")\n\n    # 2. Keyword overlap\n    kw_score = keyword_overlap_score(segment, entry.keywords)\n    if kw_score > 0.3:\n        score += kw_score * 0.35\n        matched_kw = [k for k in entry.keywords if k.lower() in segment.lower()][:2]\n        if matched_kw:\n            rationale_parts.append(f\"kw=[{','.join(matched_kw)}]\")\n\n    # 3. Standard label similarity\n    label_sim = fuzzy_similarity(segment, entry.standard_label)\n    if label_sim > 0.3:\n        score += label_sim * 0.2\n\n    # 4. Category/defect type matching\n    seg_defects = detect_defect_types(segment)\n    if entry.category_lv2 in seg_defects:\n        score += seg_defects[entry.category_lv2] * 0.25\n        rationale_parts.append(f\"defect={entry.category_lv2}\")\n\n    # 5. Component reference matching\n    seg_components = extract_component_refs(segment)\n    label_components = extract_component_refs(entry.standard_label)\n    if seg_components and label_components:\n        overlap = seg_components & label_components\n        if overlap:\n            score += 0.2\n            rationale_parts.append(f\"comp={list(overlap)[0]}\")\n\n    # 6. Signal net matching\n    seg_nets = extract_signal_nets(segment)\n    label_nets = extract_signal_nets(entry.standard_label)\n    if seg_nets and label_nets:\n        overlap = seg_nets & label_nets\n        if overlap:\n            score += 0.1\n\n    # 7. Test item alignment (weak signal)\n    if test_item:\n        test_item_upper = test_item.upper()\n        if test_item_upper in entry.standard_label.upper():\n            score += 0.05\n        elif any(net in test_item_upper for net in extract_signal_nets(entry.standard_label)):\n            score += 0.03\n\n    # Normalize score to [0, 1]\n    score = min(1.0, score)\n\n    # Build rationale\n    if rationale_parts:\n        rationale = \"; \".join(rationale_parts)\n    else:\n        rationale = \"weak_match\"\n\n    return (score, rationale)\n\n\ndef deterministic_tiebreak(candidates: List[Tuple[float, CodebookEntry, str]],\n                           record_context: dict, segment_idx: int) -> Tuple[CodebookEntry, str]:\n    \"\"\"\n    Deterministic tie-break for near-equal candidates.\n    Uses record context to make reproducible but varied selections.\n    \"\"\"\n    if len(candidates) == 1:\n        return (candidates[0][1], candidates[0][2])\n\n    # Create a deterministic hash based on context\n    context_str = f\"{record_context['record_id']}-{segment_idx}-{record_context['station']}-{record_context.get('fail_code', '')}\"\n    context_hash = int(hashlib.md5(context_str.encode()).hexdigest()[:8], 16)\n\n    # Among near-best candidates, select based on hash\n    best_score = candidates[0][0]\n    near_best = [c for c in candidates if abs(c[0] - best_score) < 0.05]\n\n    if len(near_best) > 1:\n        idx = context_hash % len(near_best)\n        return (near_best[idx][1], near_best[idx][2])\n\n    return (candidates[0][1], candidates[0][2])\n\n\ndef calibrate_confidence(match_score: float, is_unknown: bool,\n                         evidence_strength: float, segment_context: dict = None) -> float:\n    \"\"\"\n    Calibrate confidence score with proper separation between UNKNOWN and non-UNKNOWN.\n    Adds diversity based on multiple factors.\n    \"\"\"\n    # Add context-based variation\n    context_var = 0.0\n    if segment_context:\n        # Use hash of segment text for reproducible variation\n        seg_hash = hash(segment_context.get('span_text', '')) % 1000 / 10000.0\n        context_var = seg_hash\n\n    if is_unknown:\n        # UNKNOWN predictions: range [0.12, 0.48]\n        # Base varies with match score (even if below threshold)\n        base = 0.12 + match_score * 0.25\n\n        # Add variation based on evidence strength and segment characteristics\n        variation = evidence_strength * 0.08 + context_var\n        confidence = base + variation\n\n        # More diversity: check for partial matches\n        if segment_context:\n            seg_len = len(segment_context.get('span_text', ''))\n            if seg_len > 20:\n                confidence += 0.03\n            if seg_len > 40:\n                confidence += 0.02\n\n        confidence = max(0.12, min(0.48, confidence))\n    else:\n        # Non-UNKNOWN predictions: range [0.52, 0.96]\n        base = 0.52 + match_score * 0.38\n\n        # Add variation\n        variation = evidence_strength * 0.06 + context_var\n        confidence = base + variation\n\n        confidence = max(0.52, min(0.96, confidence))\n\n    return round(confidence, 4)\n\n\ndef normalize_record(row: dict, codebook_index: Dict[str, List[CodebookEntry]]) -> NormalizedRecord:\n    \"\"\"Normalize a single record from test center logs\"\"\"\n    record_id = row['record_id']\n    product_id = row['product_id']\n    station = row['station']\n    engineer_id = row['engineer_id']\n    raw_reason_text = row['raw_reason_text']\n    fail_code = row.get('fail_code', '')\n    test_item = row.get('test_item', '')\n\n    record_context = {\n        'record_id': record_id,\n        'station': station,\n        'fail_code': fail_code,\n        'test_item': test_item\n    }\n\n    result = NormalizedRecord(\n        record_id=record_id,\n        product_id=product_id,\n        station=station,\n        engineer_id=engineer_id,\n        raw_reason_text=raw_reason_text\n    )\n\n    #
